Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a elliptic algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 154. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 288/5.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to elliptic theory, we decompose the problem into 154 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 288. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
